+import SQLeanite.SQLite.Text
+
+/-! Lossless byte diagnostics carried through the existing Except String API.
+UTF-8 messages remain plain strings. Non-UTF-8 messages use a reserved NUL-led
+hex envelope until the executable writes stderr bytes. This encodes only the
+message supplied by the failing operation; it does not inspect SQL or results. -/
+namespace SQLeanite.SQLite.Diagnostic
+
+def envelope : String := "\x00SQLeanite/error-bytes/"
+
+def ofBytes (data : Array UInt8) : String :=
+  match String.fromUTF8? ⟨data⟩ with
+  | some text => if text.startsWith envelope then envelope ++ Text.hexBytes data else text
+  | none => envelope ++ Text.hexBytes data
+
+def bytes (message : String) : Array UInt8 :=
+  if message.startsWith envelope then
+    let encoded := (message.drop envelope.length).toString.toList.map Char.toNat
+    (Text.unhexCodes [] encoded #[]).getD message.toUTF8.data
+  else message.toUTF8.data
+
+theorem ofBytes_utf8 (data : Array UInt8) (message : String)
+    (valid : String.fromUTF8? ⟨data⟩ = some message)
+    (ordinary : message.startsWith envelope = false) : ofBytes data = message := by
+  simp [ofBytes, valid, ordinary]
+
+theorem bytes_ordinary (message : String)
+    (ordinary : message.startsWith envelope = false) : bytes message = message.toUTF8.data := by
+  simp [bytes, ordinary]
+
+theorem ofBytes_nonutf8 (data : Array UInt8)
+    (invalid : String.fromUTF8? ⟨data⟩ = none) : ofBytes data = envelope ++ Text.hexBytes data := by
+  simp [ofBytes, invalid]
+
+end SQLeanite.SQLite.Diagnostic
diff --git a/SQLeanite/SQLite/JsonAccess.lean b/SQLeanite/SQLite/JsonAccess.lean
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8d5e6d1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/SQLeanite/SQLite/JsonAccess.lean
@@ -0,0 +1,244 @@
+import SQLeanite.SQLite.JsonValid
+import SQLeanite.SQLite.Diagnostic
+
+/-! Read-only JSON navigation. Text is parsed to an internal binary tree;
+external JSONB retains its shallow admission and operation-local checks. -/
+namespace SQLeanite.SQLite.JsonAccess
+open JsonValid
+
+def require (message : String) (value : Option α) : Except String α :=
+  match value with | some value => .ok value | none => .error message
+
+def slice (input : Array UInt8) (start finish : Nat) : Array UInt8 :=
+  input.extract start finish
+
+def node (kind : Nat) (data : Array UInt8) : Array UInt8 :=
+  let width := if data.size < 12 then 0 else if data.size < 256 then 1
+    else if data.size < 65536 then 2 else 4
+  let tag := if width == 0 then data.size else if width == 1 then 12 else if width == 2 then 13 else 14
+  #[UInt8.ofNat (tag*16+kind)] ++
+    (List.range width).toArray.map (fun i => UInt8.ofNat (data.size / 256^(width-i-1))) ++ data
+
+structure Encoded where
+  next : Nat
+  data : Array UInt8
+
+def encodedString (input : Array UInt8) (start finish : Nat) : Array UInt8 :=
+  let data := slice input start finish
+  node (if data.any (fun c => c.toNat == 92) then 9 else 10) data
+
+mutual
+  def encode (input : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Option Encoded
+    | 0, _ => none
+    | fuel+1, pos => do
+        let pos := (skip input (input.size+1) pos false).1
+        let c := byte input pos
+        if c == 123 || c == 91 then
+          let children ← encodeMembers input fuel (c == 123) (pos+1) #[]
+          return { children with data := node (if c == 123 then 12 else 11) children.data }
+        if c == 34 || c == 39 then
+          let parsed ← string input c (input.size+1) (pos+1) (c == 39)
+          return ⟨parsed.next, encodedString input (pos+1) (parsed.next-1)⟩
+        let parsed ← if digit c || [43,45,46].contains c then number input pos else atom input pos
+        let data := slice input pos parsed.next
+        let name := data.toList.map (fun b => lower b.toNat)
+        let kind := if name == [116,114,117,101] then 1
+          else if name == [102,97,108,115,101] then 2
+          else if name == [110,117,108,108] || name.contains 110 && !name.contains 105 then 0
+          else if name.contains 105 then 5
+          else if name.contains 120 then 4
+          else if name.contains 46 || name.contains 101 then 5 else 3
+        return ⟨parsed.next, node kind (if kind ≤ 2 then #[] else data)⟩
+  def encodeMembers (input : Array UInt8) : Nat → Bool → Nat → Array UInt8 → Option Encoded
+    | 0, _, _, _ => none
+    | fuel+1, object, pos, collected => do
+        let pos := (skip input (input.size+1) pos false).1
+        let close := if object then 125 else 93
+        if byte input pos == close then return ⟨pos+1, collected⟩
+        let (pos, collected) ← if object then do
+            let c := byte input pos
+            let (finish, data) ← if c == 34 || c == 39 then do
+                let parsed ← string input c (input.size+1) (pos+1) (c == 39)
+                pure (parsed.next, encodedString input (pos+1) (parsed.next-1))
+              else do
+                let parsed ← identifier input pos
+                pure (parsed.next, encodedString input pos parsed.next)
+            let colon := (skip input (input.size+1) finish false).1
+            unless byte input colon == 58 do none
+            pure (colon+1, collected ++ data)
+          else pure (pos, collected)
+        let child ← encode input fuel pos
+        let after := (skip input (input.size+1) child.next false).1
+        let collected := collected ++ child.data
+        if byte input after == close then return ⟨after+1, collected⟩
+        unless byte input after == 44 do none
+        encodeMembers input fuel object (after+1) collected
+end
+
+def load (value : Value) : Except String (Option (Array UInt8)) := do
+  if Text.isNull value then return none
+  let input := Text.bytes value
+  if (match value with | .blob _ => blobLooks input | _ => false) then return some input
+  unless textValid input true do throw "malformed JSON"
+  match encode input (4*input.size+4) 0 with
+  | some parsed => return some parsed.data
+  | none => throw "malformed JSON"
+
+def arrayCountLoop (input : Array UInt8) (finish : Nat) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat
+  | 0, _, count => count
+  | fuel+1, pos, count =>
+      if pos ≥ finish then count else
+      match payload input pos with
+      | none => count+1
+      | some (header, size) => arrayCountLoop input finish fuel (pos+header+size) (count+1)
+
+def arrayCount (input : Array UInt8) (pos : Nat) : Nat :=
+  match payload input pos with
+  | none => 0
+  | some (header, size) => arrayCountLoop input (pos+header+size) (input.size+1) (pos+header) 0
+
+def labelChar (input : Array UInt8) (pos finish : Nat) (raw : Bool) : Nat × Nat :=
+  if pos ≥ finish then (0, pos)
+  else if !raw && byte input pos == 92 then JsonEscape.unescape input finish (input.size+1) pos
+  else JsonEscape.utf8 input pos finish
+
+def labelEqualLoop (left right : Array UInt8) (rawLeft rawRight : Bool) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Bool
+  | 0, _, _ => false
+  | fuel+1, lpos, rpos =>
+      let l := labelChar left lpos left.size rawLeft
+      let r := labelChar right rpos right.size rawRight
+      l.1 == r.1 && (l.1 == 0 || labelEqualLoop left right rawLeft rawRight fuel l.2 r.2)
+
+def labelEqual (left right : Array UInt8) (rawLeft rawRight : Bool) : Bool :=
+  if rawLeft && rawRight then left == right
+  else labelEqualLoop left right rawLeft rawRight (left.size+right.size+2) 0 0
+
+def quotedEnd (path : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Option Nat
+  | 0, _ => none
+  | fuel+1, pos =>
+      if byte path pos == 0 then none
+      else if byte path pos == 34 then some pos
+      else quotedEnd path fuel (pos + if byte path pos == 92 && byte path (pos+1) != 0 then 2 else 1)
+
+def digits (path : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat × Nat
+  | 0, pos, value => (pos, value)
+  | fuel+1, pos, value =>
+      if digit (byte path pos) then
+        digits path fuel (pos+1) (if value < 4294967295 then value*10 + byte path pos - 48 else value)
+      else (pos, value)
+
+def pathError (path : Array UInt8) : String :=
+  let data := (path.toList.takeWhile (fun c => c.toNat != 0)).flatMap
+    (fun c => if c.toNat == 39 then [c,c] else [c])
+  Diagnostic.ofBytes ("bad JSON path: '".toUTF8.data ++ data.toArray ++ #[39])
+
+mutual
+  def lookup (input path : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat → Except String (Option Nat)
+    | 0, _, _, _ => .error "JSON path too deep"
+    | fuel+1, depth, root, pos => do
+        let c := byte path pos
+        if c == 0 then return some root
+        if c == 46 then
+          let (key, after, raw) ← if byte path (pos+1) == 34 then do
+              let finish ← require (pathError path) (quotedEnd path (path.size+1) (pos+2))
+              let key := slice path (pos+2) finish
+              pure (key, finish+1, !key.any (fun c => c.toNat == 92))
+            else do
+              let finish := span path (fun c => c != 0 && c != 46 && c != 91) (path.size+1) (pos+1)
+              if finish == pos+1 then throw (pathError path)
+              pure (slice path (pos+1) finish, finish, true)
+          if byte input root % 16 != 12 then return none
+          let (header, size) := (payload input root).getD (0,0)
+          objectLookup input path fuel depth (root+header) (root+header+size) key raw after
+        else if c == 91 then
+          if byte input root % 16 != 11 then return none
+          let (header, size) := (payload input root).getD (0,0)
+          let (finish, index) := digits path (path.size+1) (pos+1) 0
+          let (finish, index) ← if finish > pos+1 && byte path finish == 93 then pure (finish, index)
+            else if byte path (pos+1) == 35 then do
+              let count := arrayCount input root
+              if byte path (pos+2) == 45 && digit (byte path (pos+3)) then
+                let (finish, back) := digits path (path.size+1) (pos+3) 0
+                if back > count then return none
+                if byte path finish != 93 then throw (pathError path)
+                pure (finish, count-back)
+              else
+                if byte path (pos+2) != 93 then throw (pathError path)
+                pure (pos+2, count)
+            else throw (pathError path)
+          arrayLookup input path fuel depth (root+header) (root+header+size) index (finish+1)
+        else throw (pathError path)
+  def objectLookup (input path : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat → Array UInt8 → Bool → Nat → Except String (Option Nat)
+    | 0, _, _, _, _, _, _ => .error "malformed JSON"
+    | fuel+1, depth, pos, finish, key, raw, after => do
+        if pos == finish then return none
+        if pos > finish then throw "malformed JSON"
+        let kind := byte input pos % 16
+        unless 7 ≤ kind && kind ≤ 10 do throw "malformed JSON"
+        let (header, size) ← require "malformed JSON" (payload input pos)
+        let value := pos+header+size
+        if value ≥ finish then throw "malformed JSON"
+        if byte input value % 16 > 12 then throw "malformed JSON"
+        let (vheader, vsize) ← require "malformed JSON" (payload input value)
+        if labelEqual key (slice input (pos+header) value) raw (kind == 7 || kind == 10) then
+          if value+vheader+vsize > finish then throw "malformed JSON"
+          if depth+1 ≥ 1000 then throw "JSON path too deep"
+          lookup input path fuel (depth+1) value after
+        else objectLookup input path fuel depth (value+vheader+vsize) finish key raw after
+  def arrayLookup (input path : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat → Except String (Option Nat)
+    | 0, _, _, _, _, _ => .error "malformed JSON"
+    | fuel+1, depth, pos, finish, index, after => do
+        if pos == finish then return none
+        if pos > finish then throw "malformed JSON"
+        if index == 0 then
+          if depth+1 ≥ 1000 then throw "JSON path too deep"
+          lookup input path fuel (depth+1) pos after
+        else
+          let (header, size) ← require "malformed JSON" (payload input pos)
+          arrayLookup input path fuel depth (pos+header+size) finish (index-1) after
+end
+
+def select (input : Array UInt8) (path : Option Value) : Except String (Option Nat) := do
+  match path with
+  | none => return some 0
+  | some .null => return none
+  | some value =>
+      let path := Text.bytes value
+      unless byte path 0 == 36 do throw (pathError path)
+      lookup input path (4*(input.size+path.size)+4) 0 0 1
+
+def typeName (kind : Nat) : String :=
+  ["null","true","false","integer","integer","real","real","text","text","text","text","array","object"].getD kind ""
+
+def evaluateType (value : Value) (path : Option Value := none) : Except String Value := do
+  let some input ← load value | return .null
+  let some pos ← select input path | return .null
+  return .text (typeName (byte input pos % 16))
+
+def evaluateLength (value : Value) (path : Option Value := none) : Except String Value := do
+  let some input ← load value | return .null
+  let some pos ← select input path | return .null
+  return .integer (if byte input pos % 16 == 11 then arrayCount input pos else 0)
+
+theorem load_null : load .null = .ok none := by
+  rfl
+
+theorem evaluateType_null (path : Option Value) : evaluateType .null path = .ok .null := by
+  rfl
+
+theorem evaluateLength_null (path : Option Value) : evaluateLength .null path = .ok .null := by
+  rfl
+
+/-- Missing paths produce SQL NULL even when the document is non-NULL. -/
+theorem evaluateType_missing (value : Value) (path : Option Value) (input : Array UInt8)
+    (loaded : load value = .ok (some input)) (missing : select input path = .ok none) :
+    evaluateType value path = .ok .null := by
+  simp [evaluateType, loaded, missing, bind, Except.bind, pure, Except.pure]
+
+/-- Counting does not require recursive validation of external JSONB. -/
+theorem arrayCountLoop_truncated (input : Array UInt8) (finish fuel pos count : Nat)
+    (inside : pos < finish) (truncated : payload input pos = none) :
+    arrayCountLoop input finish (fuel+1) pos count = count+1 := by
+  simp [arrayCountLoop, Nat.not_le_of_lt inside, truncated]
+
+end SQLeanite.SQLite.JsonAccess

+import SQLeanite.SQLite.Text
+
+/-! Byte-level SQLite JSON escape decoding, shared by validity and navigation. -/
+namespace SQLeanite.SQLite.JsonEscape
+
+def byte (input : Array UInt8) (index : Nat) : Nat := (input[index]?.getD 0).toNat
+def digit (c : Nat) : Bool := 48 ≤ c && c ≤ 57
+def lineSeparator (input : Array UInt8) (pos : Nat) : Bool :=
+  byte input pos == 226 && byte input (pos+1) == 128 &&
+    (byte input (pos+2) == 168 || byte input (pos+2) == 169)
+
+def hexInt (c : Nat) : Nat := (c + 9*((c/64)%2)) % 16
+def hex4 (input : Array UInt8) (pos : Nat) : Nat :=
+  (List.range 4).foldl (fun n i => n*16 + hexInt (byte input (pos+i))) 0
+
+/-- utf.c:sqlite3Utf8ReadLimited decodes at most four bytes without scalar
+validation. This differs intentionally from SQL unicode(). -/
+def utf8 (input : Array UInt8) (pos finish : Nat) : Nat × Nat := Id.run do
+  if pos ≥ finish then return (0, pos)
+  let c := byte input pos
+  if c < 192 then return (c, pos+1)
+  let mut value := if c < 224 then c%32 else if c < 240 then c%16
+    else if c < 248 then c%8 else if c < 252 then c%4 else if c < 254 then c%2 else 0
+  let mut next := pos+1
+  for _ in [0:3] do
+    if next < finish && byte input next / 64 == 2 then
+      value := value*64 + byte input next % 64
+      next := next+1
+    else break
+  return (value, next)
+
+def continuationEnd (input : Array UInt8) (finish : Nat) : Nat → Nat → Nat
+  | 0, pos => pos
+  | fuel+1, pos =>
+      if pos+1 ≥ finish || byte input pos != 92 then pos
+      else if byte input (pos+1) == 10 then continuationEnd input finish fuel (pos+2)
+      else if byte input (pos+1) == 13 then
+        continuationEnd input finish fuel (pos + if pos+2 < finish && byte input (pos+2) == 10 then 3 else 2)
+      else if pos+4 ≤ finish && lineSeparator input (pos+1) then continuationEnd input finish fuel (pos+4)
+      else pos
+
+def unescape (input : Array UInt8) (finish : Nat) : Nat → Nat → Nat × Nat
+  | 0, _ => (629145, finish)
+  | fuel+1, pos =>
+      if pos+1 ≥ finish then (629145, finish) else
+      let c := byte input (pos+1)
+      if c == 117 then
+        if pos+6 > finish then (629145, finish) else
+        let value := hex4 input (pos+2)
+        let low := hex4 input (pos+8)
+        if value / 1024 == 54 && pos+12 ≤ finish && byte input (pos+6) == 92 &&
+            byte input (pos+7) == 117 && low / 1024 == 55 then
+          ((value%1024)*1024 + low%1024 + 65536, pos+12)
+        else (value, pos+6)
+      else if c == 120 then
+        if pos+4 > finish then (629145, finish)
+        else (hexInt (byte input (pos+2))*16 + hexInt (byte input (pos+3)), pos+4)
+      else if c == 48 then
+        (if pos+2 < finish && digit (byte input (pos+2)) then 629145 else 0, pos+2)
+      else if [34,39,47,92].contains c then (c, pos+2)
+      else match [(98,8),(102,12),(110,10),(114,13),(116,9),(118,11)].find? (fun p => p.1 == c) with
+        | some p => (p.2, pos+2)
+        | none =>
+            let next := continuationEnd input finish (input.size+1) pos
+            if next == pos then (629145, pos+2)
+            else if next ≥ finish then (0, next)
+            else if byte input next == 92 then unescape input finish fuel next
+            else utf8 input next finish
+
+theorem unescape_short (input : Array UInt8) (finish fuel pos : Nat)
+    (short : finish ≤ pos+1) : unescape input finish (fuel+1) pos = (629145, finish) := by
+  simp [unescape, short]
+
+end SQLeanite.SQLite.JsonEscape

+/-- Preserve quoted-token diagnostics before name binding discards that
+syntax marker. The resolver supplies its actual visible column set. -/
+def nameErrorFuel (bound : Option String → String → Bool) : Nat → Expr → Option String
+  | 0, _ => none
+  | fuel+1, expression =>
+      let check := nameErrorFuel bound fuel
+      if let some name := quotedIdentifier? expression then
+        if bound none name then none
+        else some ("no such column: \"" ++ name ++ "\" - should this be a string literal in single-quotes?")
+      else match expression with
+      | .column qualifier name =>
+          if bound qualifier name then none
+          else some ("no such column: " ++ (qualifier.map (· ++ ".") |>.getD "") ++ name)
+      | .call _ arguments _ => arguments.toList.findSome? check
+      | .unary _ argument | .isNull argument _ | .collate argument _ |
+          .inSubquery argument _ _ => check argument
+      | .binary _ left right => (check left).orElse (fun _ => check right)
+      | .inList argument values _ _ => (check argument).orElse (fun _ => values.toList.findSome? check)
+      | .case_ base branches otherwise =>
+          (base.bind check).orElse (fun _ =>
+            (branches.toList.findSome? (fun pair => (check pair.1).orElse (fun _ => check pair.2))).orElse
+              (fun _ => otherwise.bind check))
+      | _ => none
+
+def nameError (bound : Option String → String → Bool) (expression : Expr) : Option String :=
+  let expression := prepare expression
+  nameErrorFuel bound expression.height expression
+
+theorem nameError_collapsed (bound : Option String → String → Bool) (expression : Expr)
+    (collapsed : prepare expression = .literal (.integer 0)) : nameError bound expression = none := by
+  simp [nameError, collapsed, nameErrorFuel, quotedIdentifier?, Expr.height]
+

+private def expectSQLiteJsonAccess : IO Unit := do
+  -- 5,343 pinned C controls; the original component diagnostics and all inputs
+  -- are retained separately from production executable evaluations.
+  let cases := jsonAccessControls0 ++ jsonAccessControls1 ++ jsonAccessControls2 ++ jsonAccessControls3 ++ jsonAccessControls4 ++ jsonAccessControls5 ++ jsonAccessControls6 ++ jsonAccessControls7 ++ jsonAccessControls8
+  for (sql, expected, expectedError) in cases do
+    match CoreSQL.execute Core.State.empty sql with
+    | .error message => unless expectedError == some message do fail s!"JSON access error differs: {sql}: {message}"
+    | .ok (_, results) =>
+        if expectedError.isSome then fail s!"JSON access unexpectedly succeeded: {sql}"
+        let wanted ← match expected.mapM SQLSurface.value with
+          | .ok row => pure row
+          | .error message => fail message
+        unless (results.getLast?).map (·.rows) == some [wanted] do
+          fail s!"JSON access differs from C: {sql}"
+
+private def expectSQLiteByteDiagnostics : IO Unit := do
+  -- All 65,536 two-byte strings exercise lossless diagnostic transport,
+  -- including malformed UTF-8 and embedded NUL. This is a component control.
+  for high in [0:256] do
+    for low in [0:256] do
+      let data := #[UInt8.ofNat high, UInt8.ofNat low]
+      unless SQLite.Diagnostic.bytes (SQLite.Diagnostic.ofBytes data) == data do
+        fail "diagnostic byte roundtrip"
+  let marker := SQLite.Diagnostic.envelope.toUTF8.data
+  unless SQLite.Diagnostic.bytes (SQLite.Diagnostic.ofBytes marker) == marker do
+    fail "diagnostic envelope collision"
+  for fn in ["json_type", "json_array_length"] do
+    let sql := "SELECT " ++ fn ++ "('0',CAST(X'24ff' AS TEXT));"
+    match CoreSQL.execute Core.State.empty sql with
+    | .error message =>
+        unless SQLite.Diagnostic.bytes message == "bad JSON path: '$".toUTF8.data ++ #[255,39] do
+          fail "JSON path diagnostic lost invalid UTF-8"
+    | .ok _ => fail "invalid JSON path was admitted"
+
+private def expectSQLiteIdenticalUpdates : IO Unit := do
+  let cases : List (String × String × Bool) := [
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET v=v;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET v=NULL WHERE id=1;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET w=w;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET id=id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET v=v RETURNING id;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE 0;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=NULL WHERE id=1;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET w=w;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET id=id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v RETURNING id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE 0;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=NULL WHERE id=1;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET w=w;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET id=id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v RETURNING id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE 0;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET v=v;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET v=NULL WHERE id=1;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET w=w;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET id=id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET v=v RETURNING id;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE 0;", true)]
+  for (setup, sql, unchanged) in cases do
+    let (before, _) ← match CoreSQL.execute Core.State.empty setup with
+      | .ok x => pure x | .error message => fail message
+    let statements ← match CoreTokenGrammar.parseStatements sql with
+      | .ok x => pure x | .error message => fail message
+    let statement ← match statements[0]? with
+      | some statement => pure statement | none => fail "missing UPDATE statement"
+    let (after, results) ← match CoreSQL.executeStatement before statement with
+      | .ok x => pure x | .error message => fail message
+    unless CoreFileEngine.dataWriteNoop before after statement results == unchanged do
+      fail s!"identical UPDATE publication disagrees with C: {setup} {sql}"
+

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Compare JSON type/array length and path lookup through both public file engines.
+
+New seeded documents, paths and binary mutations mix with fixed syntax, depth,
+arity and query-shape controls. Complete stdout/status and normalized first-line
+errors are compared. File reads must preserve main/WAL bytes; independent C
+checks integrity and typed rows. No oracle outcomes filter the generated cases.
+"""
+import argparse,hashlib,json,os,random,re,shutil,subprocess,tempfile,importlib.util
+from pathlib import Path
+ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+def digest(root):
+ h=hashlib.sha256()
+ for p in sorted((root/'SQLeanite').rglob('*.lean'))+[root/'Main.lean']:h.update(str(p.relative_to(root)).encode()+b'\0'+p.read_bytes())
+ return h.hexdigest()
+def literal(s):
+ data=s.encode() if isinstance(s,str) else s
+ return "CAST(X'"+data.hex()+"' AS TEXT)"
+def blob(data):return "X'"+data.hex()+"'"
+def node(kind,data):
+ n=len(data);w=0 if n<12 else 1 if n<256 else 2 if n<65536 else 4
+ return bytes([(n if not w else {1:12,2:13,4:14}[w])*16+kind])+(n.to_bytes(w,'big') if w else b'')+data
+def error(r):
+ if not r['code']:return ''
+ line=r['stderr'].splitlines()[0] if r['stderr'] else ''
+ return re.sub(r'^(?:Parse error|Runtime error|Error)(?: near line [0-9]+)?: ?', '',line)
+def main():
+ p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__);p.add_argument('--seed',type=int,default=354802);p.add_argument('--fixtures',type=int,default=2);p.add_argument('--random-cases',type=int,default=64);p.add_argument('--controls',type=Path);p.add_argument('--implementation-root',type=Path,default=ROOT);p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);a=p.parse_args();rng=random.Random(a.seed)
+ root=a.implementation_root.resolve();binary=root/'.lake/build/bin/sqleanite';sqlite=os.environ.get('SQLITE3_BIN','sqlite3');source=digest(root);exe=hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+ def run(cmd,sql):
+  r=subprocess.run(cmd,input=sql.encode(),capture_output=True,timeout=120)
+  return dict(code=r.returncode,stdout=r.stdout.decode('latin1'),stderr=r.stderr.decode('latin1'))
+ def csql(path,sql):return run([sqlite,'-batch','-bail',str(path)],sql)
+ def snapshot(path):return {s:hashlib.sha256(Path(str(path)+s).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for s in ['','-wal'] if Path(str(path)+s).exists()}
+ basecases=[]
+ def add(group,value,path=None):
+  args=value+('' if path is None else ','+path)
+  for fn in ['json_type','json_array_length']:
+   expr=f'{fn}({args})';basecases.append(dict(group=group,sql=f'SELECT typeof({expr}),quote({expr});'))
+ paths=['$', '$.a', '$[0]', '$[#-1]', '$[#]', '$[#-999bad]', '$[9999999999999999]', '$.missing[bad]', '$.','$."','$."\\u0061"','$."a\\u0000b"']
+ for value in ['null','1','1e2','+0xabc','NaN','Infinity','[]','{}','[1,{},[2]]','{"a":[1,2],"a":0}',"{a:[1,],b:'x'}",'"a"','invalid','[','']:
+  for path in [None]+paths:add('text-path-controls',literal(value),None if path is None else literal(path))
+ for value in ['NULL','0','1.25','1e999',blob(b'null'),blob(b'1234')]:
+  for path in [None,'NULL','0',blob(b'$')]:add('sql-storage',value,path)
+ for kind in range(16):
+  for data in [b'',b'\xff',b'\x17a\x00',b'\x13a\x00']:
+   for path in [None,literal('$[0]'),literal('$.a')]:add('binary-tag',blob(node(kind,data)),path)
+ for depth in [999,1000,1001]:
+  text='['*depth+'0'+']'*depth;data=b'\x13\x30'
+  for _ in range(depth):data=node(11,data)
+  for val in [literal(text),blob(data)]:
+   for path in [None,literal('$'+'[0]'*(depth-1)),literal('$'+'[0]'*depth)]:add('depth',val,path)
+ for value in [literal('0'),literal('{}'),literal('{"a":1}')]:
+  for path in [b'$.\xff',b'$\xff',b'$."\xff',b'$\0ignored',b"x'y"]:add('path-bytes',value,literal(path))
+ if a.controls:
+  for c in json.loads(a.controls.read_text())['cases']:add('fixed-component-replay',c['sql_value'],c['sql_path'])
+ for fn in ['json_type','json_array_length']:
+  for call in [fn+'()',fn.upper()+'(1,2,3)',fn+'(*)']:
+   for expr in [call,'0 AND '+call,'CASE WHEN 0 THEN '+call+' ELSE 1 END','1 IN ('+call+')']:
+    basecases.append(dict(group='preparation',sql='SELECT '+expr+' FROM t LIMIT 0;'))
+  for q in [f'SELECT id,{fn}(v) FROM t;',f'SELECT id,{fn}(v,p) FROM t ORDER BY id;',f'SELECT {fn}(v) FROM t WHERE id<6 ORDER BY {fn}(v);',f'SELECT {fn}(v),count(*) FROM t WHERE id<6 GROUP BY {fn}(v);',f'SELECT sum({fn}(v)) FROM t;',f'SELECT {fn}(NULL,abs(-9223372036854775808));',f'SELECT {fn}("bad",NULL);']:
+   basecases.append(dict(group='query-shape',sql=q))
+  for args in ["'bad',NULL", "NULL,'bad'", "'[',NULL"]:
+   basecases.append(dict(group='null-path-error-order',sql=f'SELECT {fn}({args});'))
+  for count in [1000,1001]:
+   basecases.append(dict(group='argument-limit',sql='SELECT '+fn+'('+','.join(['1']*count)+');'))
+
+ def random_value(depth=0):
+  pick=rng.randrange(8 if depth<3 else 6)
+  if pick==0:return None
+  if pick==1:return rng.choice([True,False])
+  if pick==2:return rng.randrange(-10**15,10**15)
+  if pick==3:return rng.random()*10**rng.randrange(-15,15)
+  if pick<6:return ''.join(rng.choice('abé中😀\\\n\t"0123') for _ in range(rng.randrange(16)))
+  if pick==6:return [random_value(depth+1) for _ in range(rng.randrange(5))]
+  return {''.join(rng.choice('abé中😀\\\n\t".0123') for _ in range(rng.randrange(5))):random_value(depth+1) for _ in range(rng.randrange(5))}
+ def actual_paths(value,path='$'):
+  out=[path]
+  if isinstance(value,list):
+   for i,v in enumerate(value):out+=actual_paths(v,path+f'[{i}]')
+  elif isinstance(value,dict):
+   for k,v in value.items():out+=actual_paths(v,path+'.'+json.dumps(k,ensure_ascii=bool(rng.randrange(2))))
+  return out
+ cases=[];fixtures=[];generated=[]
+ with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='sqleanite-json-access-') as tmp:
+  for fixture in range(a.fixtures):
+   queries=list(basecases)
+   for _ in range(a.random_cases):
+    value=random_value();text=json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=bool(rng.randrange(2)),separators=(',',':'));paths2=actual_paths(value)
+    result=csql(':memory:','SELECT hex(jsonb('+literal(text)+'));');assert result['code']==0,result
+    data=bytes.fromhex(result['stdout'].strip());path=rng.choice(paths2)
+    if rng.randrange(3)==0:path+=rng.choice(['.a','[0]','[#-1]','."','[bad]','xxx'])
+    mutations=bytearray(data)
+    pos=rng.randrange(len(mutations)+1);op=rng.randrange(3)
+    if op==0:mutations[pos:pos]=bytes([rng.randrange(256)])
+    elif pos<len(mutations) and op==1:del mutations[pos]
+    elif pos<len(mutations):mutations[pos]=rng.randrange(256)
+    generated.append(dict(fixture=fixture,text=text,jsonb=data.hex(),path=path,mutation=mutations.hex(),oracle=result))
+    for val in [literal(text),blob(data),blob(mutations)]:
+     for fn in ['json_type','json_array_length']:
+      expr=fn+'('+val+','+literal(path)+')';queries.append(dict(group='seeded-input-path',sql=f'SELECT typeof({expr}),quote({expr});'))
+   page=512<<(fixture%8);folder=Path(tmp)/str(fixture);folder.mkdir();base=folder/'base.db'
+   setup=f'PRAGMA page_size={page};CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,p);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,\'null\',\'$\'),(2,\'[1,2]\',\'$[0]\'),(3,\'{{"a":[3]}}\',\'$.a\'),(4,NULL,NULL),(5,X\'0b\',\'$[#-1]\'),(6,\'bad\',\'$\');'
+   made=csql(base,setup);assert made['code']==0,made
+   for mode in ['--verified-file','--verified-wal-file']:
+    left=folder/(mode+'-lean.db');right=folder/(mode+'-c.db');shutil.copyfile(base,left);shutil.copyfile(base,right);bl,br=snapshot(left),snapshot(right)
+    for q in queries:
+     l=run([str(binary),mode,str(left)],q['sql']);c=csql(right,q['sql'])
+     cases.append(dict(q,fixture=fixture,page_size=page,mode=mode,lean=l,sqlite=c,lean_error=error(l),sqlite_error=error(c),matched=bool(l['code'])==bool(c['code']) and l['stdout']==c['stdout'] and error(l)==error(c)))
+    obs='PRAGMA integrity_check;SELECT id,typeof(v),hex(v),typeof(p),hex(p) FROM t ORDER BY id;';lo,co=csql(left,obs),csql(right,obs)
+    fixtures.append(dict(fixture=fixture,mode=mode,page_size=page,setup=setup,lean_unchanged=bl==snapshot(left),sqlite_unchanged=br==snapshot(right),observation_lean=lo,observation_sqlite=co,matched=bl==snapshot(left) and br==snapshot(right) and lo['code']==co['code']==0 and lo['stdout']==co['stdout'] and lo['stdout'].startswith('ok\n')))
+ assert source==digest(root) and exe==hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'Implementation changed during evaluation'
+ failures=sum(not c['matched'] for c in cases)+sum(not c['matched'] for c in fixtures)
+ a.output.write_text(json.dumps(dict(seed=a.seed,random_cases=a.random_cases,controls=str(a.controls),cases=cases,fixtures=fixtures,generated=generated,failures=failures,comparison=__doc__.strip(),implementation_sha256=source,executable_sha256=exe,harness_sha256=hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),sqlite_version=subprocess.check_output([sqlite,'--version'],text=True).strip()),indent=2)+'\n')
+ print(json.dumps(dict(cases=len(cases),failures=failures)));return bool(failures)
+if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""JSON type/array-length controls from pinned SQLite's randomjson test-data generator.
+
+Only the C oracle process loads the upstream extension. Production never calls
+the generator, C, Python, or an external parser. Four input representations per
+seed (JSON text, JSON5 text, JSONB of each) pass through both public file modes.
+Root, first/last array elements and object paths are compared, with immutable
+main/WAL bytes and independent C typed-row/integrity observations.
+"""
+import argparse,hashlib,json,os,shutil,subprocess,tempfile,time
+from pathlib import Path
+ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+def digest():
+ h=hashlib.sha256()
+ for p in sorted((ROOT/'SQLeanite').rglob('*.lean'))+[ROOT/'Main.lean']:h.update(str(p.relative_to(ROOT)).encode()+b'\0'+p.read_bytes())
+ return h.hexdigest()
+def main():
+ p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__);p.add_argument('--start-seed',type=int,default=1);p.add_argument('--count',type=int,default=5000);p.add_argument('--page-size',type=int,default=4096);p.add_argument('--extension',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--generator-source',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);a=p.parse_args()
+ sqlite=os.environ.get('SQLITE3_BIN','sqlite3');binary=ROOT/'.lake/build/bin/sqleanite';source=digest();exe=sha(binary)
+ def run(cmd,sql):
+  start=time.time();r=subprocess.run(cmd,input=sql.encode(),capture_output=True,timeout=600)
+  return dict(code=r.returncode,stdout=r.stdout.decode('latin1'),stderr=r.stderr.decode('latin1'),seconds=time.time()-start)
+ def csql(path,sql):return run([sqlite,'-batch','-bail',str(path)],sql)
+ def snapshot(path):return {s:sha(Path(str(path)+s)) for s in ['','-wal'] if Path(str(path)+s).exists()}
+ cases=[];runs=[]
+ with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='sqleanite-json-upstream-') as tmp:
+  folder=Path(tmp);base=folder/'base.db'
+  setup=f'.load {a.extension} sqlite3_randomjson_init\nPRAGMA page_size={a.page_size};CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v);\n'
+  for offset,expr in enumerate(['random_json(seed)','random_json5(seed)','jsonb(random_json(seed))','jsonb(random_json5(seed))']):
+   setup+=f'WITH RECURSIVE seeds(seed) AS (VALUES({a.start_seed}) UNION ALL SELECT seed+1 FROM seeds WHERE seed<{a.start_seed+a.count-1}) INSERT INTO t SELECT seed*4+{offset},{expr} FROM seeds;\n'
+  generated=csql(base,setup);assert generated['code']==0,generated
+  values=csql(base,'SELECT id,typeof(v),hex(v) FROM t ORDER BY id;');assert values['code']==0,values
+  inputs=values['stdout'].splitlines();assert len(inputs)==4*a.count
+  paths=[None,'$','$[0]','$[#-1]','$.a','$.x','$[0].a[0]']
+  fields=["quote("+fn+"(v"+("" if path is None else ",'"+path+"'")+"))" for fn in ['json_type','json_array_length'] for path in paths]
+  sql='SELECT id,'+','.join(fields)+' FROM t ORDER BY id;'
+  for mode in ['--verified-file','--verified-wal-file']:
+   left=folder/(mode+'-lean.db');right=folder/(mode+'-c.db');shutil.copyfile(base,left);shutil.copyfile(base,right);bl,br=snapshot(left),snapshot(right)
+   l=run([str(binary),mode,str(left)],sql);c=csql(right,sql);ll,cl=l['stdout'].splitlines(),c['stdout'].splitlines()
+   assert len(cl)==len(inputs),c
+   for i,wanted in enumerate(cl):
+    actual=ll[i] if i<len(ll) else None;identifier,kind,hexvalue=inputs[i].split('|')
+    cases.append(dict(mode=mode,seed=int(identifier)//4,representation=int(identifier)%4,input_type=kind,input_hex=hexvalue,lean=actual,sqlite=wanted,matched=actual==wanted))
+   lo=csql(left,'PRAGMA integrity_check;SELECT id,typeof(v),hex(v) FROM t ORDER BY id;');co=csql(right,'PRAGMA integrity_check;SELECT id,typeof(v),hex(v) FROM t ORDER BY id;')
+   runs.append(dict(mode=mode,sql=sql,lean=l,sqlite=c,lean_unchanged=bl==snapshot(left),sqlite_unchanged=br==snapshot(right),observation_lean=lo,observation_sqlite=co,
+     matched=l['code']==c['code']==0 and l['stdout']==c['stdout'] and len(ll)==len(cl) and bl==snapshot(left) and br==snapshot(right) and lo['code']==co['code']==0 and lo['stdout']==co['stdout'] and lo['stdout'].startswith('ok\n')))
+ assert source==digest() and exe==sha(binary),'Implementation changed during evaluation'
+ failures=sum(not c['matched'] for c in cases)+sum(not r['matched'] for r in runs)
+ report=dict(start_seed=a.start_seed,count=a.count,page_size=a.page_size,generator_source=str(a.generator_source),generator_source_sha256=sha(a.generator_source),extension_sha256=sha(a.extension),generation_sql=setup,comparison=__doc__.strip(),
+  cases=cases,runs=runs,failures=failures,implementation_sha256=source,executable_sha256=exe,harness_sha256=sha(Path(__file__)),sqlite_version=subprocess.check_output([sqlite,'--version'],text=True).strip())
+ a.output.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(dict(cases=len(cases),failures=failures)));return bool(failures)
+if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Persist json_type()/json_array_length() results and failures in both public Core file engines.
+
+Pinned C supplies page geometry and input JSONB. Newly seeded data and SQL
+variants mix with fixed transaction/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/RETURNING/CTAS
+templates. Compare full stdout, normalized first-line error, post-statement
+C typed rows/catalog/integrity, Lean reopen, and a subsequent independent C
+write. Preserve unchanged files when C leaves its input unchanged. Page size
+is recorded separately because the current writer may convert it to 4096.
+"""
+import argparse,hashlib,json,os,random,re,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+def digest():
+ h=hashlib.sha256()
+ for p in sorted((ROOT/'SQLeanite').rglob('*.lean'))+[ROOT/'Main.lean']:h.update(str(p.relative_to(ROOT)).encode()+b'\0'+p.read_bytes())
+ return h.hexdigest()
+def literal(s):
+ data=s.encode() if isinstance(s,str) else s
+ return "CAST(X'"+data.hex()+"' AS TEXT)"
+def main():
+ p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__);p.add_argument('--seed',type=int,default=354803);p.add_argument('--fixtures',type=int,default=4);p.add_argument('--random-cases',type=int,default=16);p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);a=p.parse_args();rng=random.Random(a.seed)
+ sqlite=os.environ.get('SQLITE3_BIN','sqlite3');binary=ROOT/'.lake/build/bin/sqleanite';source=digest();exe=hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+ def run(cmd,sql):
+  r=subprocess.run(cmd,input=sql.encode(),capture_output=True,timeout=120)
+  return dict(code=r.returncode,stdout=r.stdout.decode('latin1'),stderr=r.stderr.decode('latin1'))
+ def csql(path,sql):return run([sqlite,'-batch','-bail',str(path)],sql)
+ def error(r):
+  if not r['code']:return ''
+  line=r['stderr'].splitlines()[0] if r['stderr'] else ''
+  return re.sub(r'^(?:Parse error|Runtime error|Error)(?: near line [0-9]+)?: ?', '',line)
+ def snapshot(path):
+  return {s:hashlib.sha256(Path(str(path)+s).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for s in ['','-wal'] if Path(str(path)+s).exists()}
+ def same(l,r):return bool(l['code'])==bool(r['code']) and l['stdout']==r['stdout'] and error(l)==error(r)
+ inputs=[literal(s) for s in ['null','1','[]','{"a":[1,true]}','{a:1}','[1,]','"\\x41"','invalid','[','01','1\0junk']]
+ inputs += ['NULL','1','1.25',"X'00'","X'0b'","X'3c176100'","X'1bff'","X'c000'","X'00ff'"]
+ generated=[]
+ for _ in range(a.random_cases):
+  value={'v':[rng.randrange(-100000,100000),rng.choice([None,True,False]),''.join(rng.choice('abé中😀') for _ in range(5))]}
+  s=json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=False);r=csql(':memory:','SELECT hex(jsonb('+literal(s)+'));');assert r['code']==0,r
+  generated.append(dict(text=s,jsonb_hex=r['stdout'].strip()))
+  inputs.extend([literal(s),"X'"+r['stdout'].strip()+"'"])
+ scripts=[]
+ def add(group,sql):scripts.append(dict(group=group,sql=sql))
+ for mask in [None,"'$'","'$.v'","'$[#-1]'",'flags']:
+  for fn in ['json_type','json_array_length']:
+   call=fn+'(v'+('' if mask is None else ','+str(mask))+')'
+   add('update',f'UPDATE t SET valid={call};')
+   add('insert-select',f'INSERT INTO out SELECT id,{call} FROM t;')
+   add('ctas',f'CREATE TABLE made AS SELECT id,{call} AS valid FROM t;')
+   add('delete',f'DELETE FROM t WHERE NOT {call};')
+   add('update-valid',f'UPDATE t SET valid={call} WHERE id<=7 OR id>20;')
+   add('insert-valid',f'INSERT INTO out SELECT id,{call} FROM t WHERE id<=7 OR id>20;')
+   add('ctas-valid',f'CREATE TABLE made AS SELECT id,{call} AS valid FROM t WHERE id<=7 OR id>20;')
+   add('delete-valid',f'DELETE FROM t WHERE (id<=7 OR id>20) AND NOT {call};')
+ for suffix in ['ROLLBACK;','COMMIT;']:
+  add('transaction','BEGIN;\nUPDATE t SET valid=json_array_length(v) WHERE id<=7 OR id>20;\n'+suffix+'\nSELECT id,valid FROM t ORDER BY id;')
+ add('savepoint','SAVEPOINT j;\nUPDATE t SET valid=json_type(v) WHERE id<=7 OR id>20;\nROLLBACK TO j;\nRELEASE j;\nSELECT id,valid FROM t ORDER BY id;')
+ add('atomic-error',"UPDATE t SET valid=json_type(v,CASE WHEN id=3 THEN 'bad' ELSE '$' END);")
+ add('transaction-error',"BEGIN;\nUPDATE t SET valid=json_array_length(v) WHERE id<=7 OR id>20;\nUPDATE t SET valid=json_type(v,CASE WHEN id=3 THEN 'bad' ELSE '$' END);\nCOMMIT;")
+ add('update-returning','UPDATE t SET valid=json_array_length(v) WHERE id<5 RETURNING id,valid;')
+ add('returning-error',"UPDATE t SET valid=1 WHERE id<5 RETURNING id,json_type(v,CASE WHEN id=3 THEN 'bad' ELSE '$' END);")
+ add('insert-returning',"INSERT INTO t VALUES(999,'[1,]',2,json_array_length('[1,]')) RETURNING id,valid;")
+ add('admission-error','UPDATE t SET valid=json_type() WHERE 0;')
+ add('admission-error','INSERT INTO out SELECT id,json_array_length(1,2,3) FROM t WHERE 0;')
+ for _ in range(a.random_cases):

+  add('seeded-update',f'UPDATE t SET valid={fn}(v,{mask}) WHERE id BETWEEN {lo} AND {hi};')

+ read='SELECT id,typeof(v),hex(v),typeof(flags),quote(flags),typeof(valid),quote(valid) FROM t ORDER BY id;SELECT id,typeof(valid),quote(valid) FROM out ORDER BY id;'
+ cases=[];fixtures=[]

+  for fixture in range(a.fixtures):
+   folder=Path(tmp)/str(fixture);folder.mkdir();base=folder/'base.db';page=512<<(fixture%8)
+   setup=f'PRAGMA page_size={page};CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,flags,valid);CREATE TABLE out(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,valid);'
+   for i,value in enumerate(inputs):
+    flags=rng.choice(["'$'","'$.v'","'$[0]'","'$[#-1]'","'bad'",'NULL'])
+    setup+=f'INSERT INTO t VALUES({i+1},{value},{flags},NULL);'
+   created=csql(base,setup);assert created['code']==0,created
+   fixtures.append(dict(fixture=fixture,page_size=page,setup=setup))
+   for mode in ['--verified-file','--verified-wal-file']:
+    for index,script in enumerate(scripts):
+     case_dir=folder/(mode+str(index));case_dir.mkdir();left=case_dir/'lean.db';right=case_dir/'sqlite.db';shutil.copyfile(base,left);shutil.copyfile(base,right)

+     matched=same(l,c) and lo['code']==co['code']==0 and lo['stdout']==co['stdout'] and lo['stdout'].startswith('ok\n') and same(lr,cr) and lw['code']==cw['code']==0 and lw['stdout']==cw['stdout'] and (ar!=br or al==bl)
+     cases.append(dict(script,fixture=fixture,page_size=page,mode=mode,lean=l,sqlite=c,lean_error=error(l),sqlite_error=error(c),observation_lean=lo,observation_sqlite=co,reopen_lean=lr,reopen_sqlite=cr,subsequent_c_write_lean=lw,subsequent_c_write_sqlite=cw,geometry=geometry,lean_unchanged=al==bl,sqlite_unchanged=ar==br,matched=matched))
+ assert source==digest() and exe==hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'Implementation changed during evaluation'

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Binary JSON escape controls plus separately counted fresh seeded payloads.
+
+Enumerate every byte after backslash, continuation/Unicode sentinel boundaries,
+four text kinds, nonminimal headers and enclosing arrays/objects. Compare all
+json_valid masks and default. Root type/length are conditional SQL expressions
+guarded by each implementation's own superficial check, not oracle filtering.
+Preserve all rows, input bytes, mode status, immutable files and C observations.
+"""
+import argparse,hashlib,json,os,random,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+def digest():
+ h=hashlib.sha256()
+ for p in sorted((ROOT/'SQLeanite').rglob('*.lean'))+[ROOT/'Main.lean']:h.update(str(p.relative_to(ROOT)).encode()+b'\0'+p.read_bytes())
+ return h.hexdigest()
+def node(kind,data,width=None):
+ n=len(data)
+ if width is None:width=0 if n<12 else 1 if n<256 else 2 if n<65536 else 4
+ return bytes([(n if width==0 else {1:12,2:13,4:14,8:15}[width])*16+kind])+(n.to_bytes(width,'big') if width else b'')+data
+def main():
+ p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__);p.add_argument('--seed',type=int,default=354831);p.add_argument('--random-cases',type=int,default=128);p.add_argument('--fixtures',type=int,default=2);p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);a=p.parse_args();rng=random.Random(a.seed)
+ sqlite=os.environ.get('SQLITE3_BIN','sqlite3');binary=ROOT/'.lake/build/bin/sqleanite';source=digest();exe=hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest();values=[]
+ def add(group,kind,data,width=None):
+  b=node(kind,data,width)
+  for shape,value in [('root',b),('array',node(11,b)),('object',node(12,b'\x17a'+b))]:values.append(dict(group=group,shape=shape,hex=value.hex()))
+ for kind in [7,8,9,10]:
+  for c in range(256):
+   for prefix in [b'',b'a',b'\\\n',b'\\\r\n']:
+    for suffix in [b'',b'a']:
+     data=prefix+b'\\'+bytes([c])+suffix;width=[0,1,2,4,8][(c+kind+len(prefix)+len(suffix))%5];add('fixed-byte-escape',kind,data,width)
+ for value in [0,127,128,0xd800,0xdc00,0xfffe,0xffff,0x99998,0x99999,0x9999a,0x10ffff]:
+  data=chr(value).encode('utf-8','surrogatepass')
+  for kind in [7,8,9,10]:
+   for prefix in [b'',b'\\\n',b'\\\r',b'\\\r\n',b'\\\xe2\x80\xa8',b'\\\xe2\x80\xa9']:
+    add('fixed-unicode-boundary',kind,prefix+data)
+ fixed=len(values)
+ for _ in range(a.random_cases):
+  data=bytearray(bytes(rng.randrange(256) for _ in range(rng.randrange(1,32))));pos=rng.randrange(len(data)+1)
+  data[pos:pos]=rng.choice([b'\\',b'\\\n',b'\\u',b'\\x',b'\\\r\n']);add('seeded-payload',rng.randrange(7,11),data)
+ def run(cmd,sql):
+  r=subprocess.run(cmd,input=sql.encode(),capture_output=True,timeout=600);return dict(code=r.returncode,stdout=r.stdout.decode('latin1'),stderr=r.stderr.decode('latin1'))
+ def csql(path,sql):return run([sqlite,'-batch','-bail',str(path)],sql)
+ def snap(path):return {s:hashlib.sha256(Path(str(path)+s).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for s in ['','-wal'] if Path(str(path)+s).exists()}
+ cases=[];runs=[]
+ sql='SELECT id,'+','.join(['json_valid(v)']+[f'json_valid(v,{n})' for n in range(1,16)]+["CASE WHEN json_valid(v,4) THEN quote(json_type(v)) END","CASE WHEN json_valid(v,4) THEN quote(json_array_length(v)) END"])+ ' FROM t ORDER BY id;'
+ with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='sqleanite-json-escapes-') as tmp:
+  for fixture in range(a.fixtures):
+   page=[512,65536,1024,32768,2048,16384,4096,8192][fixture%8];folder=Path(tmp)/str(fixture);folder.mkdir();base=folder/'base.db'
+   setup=f'PRAGMA page_size={page};CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v);BEGIN;'+''.join(f"INSERT INTO t VALUES({i},X'{v['hex']}');" for i,v in enumerate(values))+'COMMIT;';made=csql(base,setup);assert made['code']==0,made
+   for mode in ['--verified-file','--verified-wal-file']:
+    left=folder/(mode+'-lean.db');right=folder/(mode+'-c.db');shutil.copyfile(base,left);shutil.copyfile(base,right);bl,br=snap(left),snap(right)
+    l=run([str(binary),mode,str(left)],sql);c=csql(right,sql);ll,cl=l['stdout'].splitlines(),c['stdout'].splitlines();assert len(cl)==len(values),c
+    for i,wanted in enumerate(cl):
+     actual=ll[i] if i<len(ll) else None;cases.append(dict(fixture=fixture,page_size=page,mode=mode,input=i,group=values[i]['group'],lean=actual,sqlite=wanted,matched=actual==wanted))
+    obs='PRAGMA integrity_check;SELECT id,hex(v) FROM t ORDER BY id;';lo,co=csql(left,obs),csql(right,obs)
+    runs.append(dict(fixture=fixture,page_size=page,mode=mode,sql=sql,lean=l,sqlite=c,lean_unchanged=bl==snap(left),sqlite_unchanged=br==snap(right),observation_lean=lo,observation_sqlite=co,matched=l['code']==c['code']==0 and l['stdout']==c['stdout'] and bl==snap(left) and br==snap(right) and lo['code']==co['code']==0 and lo['stdout']==co['stdout'] and lo['stdout'].startswith('ok\n')))
+ assert source==digest() and exe==hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+ failures=sum(not c['matched'] for c in cases)+sum(not c['matched'] for c in runs)
+ report=dict(seed=a.seed,random_cases=a.random_cases,values=values,fixed_inputs=fixed,seeded_inputs=len(values)-fixed,cases=cases,runs=runs,failures=failures,implementation_sha256=source,executable_sha256=exe,harness_sha256=hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),sqlite_version=subprocess.check_output([sqlite,'--version'],text=True).strip(),comparison=__doc__.strip())
+ a.output.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(dict(cases=len(cases),failures=failures,fixed_inputs=fixed,seeded_inputs=len(values)-fixed)));return bool(failures)
+if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Fresh identical-UPDATE publication controls, including changed indexed keys.
+
+Compare complete stdout/status/error, independent C typed rows/integrity, and
+whether both main/WAL byte sets stay unchanged. An unchanged row image does not
+justify skipping indexed-column or rowid writes. Both public modes/eight page
+sizes are exercised by the full fixture matrix; all cases are retained.
+"""
+import argparse,hashlib,json,os,random,re,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+def digest():
+ h=hashlib.sha256()
+ for p in sorted((ROOT/'SQLeanite').rglob('*.lean'))+[ROOT/'Main.lean']:h.update(str(p.relative_to(ROOT)).encode()+b'\0'+p.read_bytes())
+ return h.hexdigest()
+def main():
+ p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__);p.add_argument('--seed',type=int,default=354805);p.add_argument('--fixtures',type=int,default=8);p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);a=p.parse_args();rng=random.Random(a.seed)
+ sqlite=os.environ.get('SQLITE3_BIN','sqlite3');binary=ROOT/'.lake/build/bin/sqleanite';source=digest();exe=hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest();cases=[];fixtures=[]
+ def run(cmd,sql):
+  r=subprocess.run(cmd,input=sql.encode(),capture_output=True,timeout=60);return dict(code=r.returncode,stdout=r.stdout.decode('latin1'),stderr=r.stderr.decode('latin1'))
+ def csql(path,sql):return run([sqlite,'-batch','-bail',str(path)],sql)
+ def snap(path):return {s:hashlib.sha256(Path(str(path)+s).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for s in ['','-wal'] if Path(str(path)+s).exists()}
+ def err(r):return re.sub(r'^(?:Parse error|Runtime error|Error)(?: near line [0-9]+)?: ?', '',r['stderr'].splitlines()[0]) if r['code'] and r['stderr'] else ''
+ affinities=['','TEXT','INTEGER','NUMERIC','REAL'];rng.shuffle(affinities)
+ with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='sqleanite-update-noop-') as tmp:
+  for fixture in range(a.fixtures):
+   page=512<<(fixture%8);decl=affinities[fixture%len(affinities)]
+   vals=['NULL',str(rng.randrange(-10000,10000)),str(rng.random()*1000),"'"+str(rng.randrange(10**9))+"'","X'"+bytes(rng.randrange(256) for _ in range(5)).hex()+"'"]
+   for ix,index in enumerate(['','CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);','CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);','CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);']):
+    folder=Path(tmp)/f'{fixture}-{ix}';folder.mkdir();base=folder/'base.db'

+    made=csql(base,setup);assert made['code']==0,made;fixtures.append(dict(fixture=fixture,index=ix,page_size=page,setup=setup))
+    sqls=['UPDATE t SET v=v;','UPDATE t SET v=NULL WHERE id=1;','UPDATE t SET w=w;','UPDATE t SET id=id;','UPDATE t SET rowid=rowid;','UPDATE t SET v=v RETURNING id;','UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE 0;','UPDATE t SET v=v,w=w;',"UPDATE t SET v=CASE WHEN id=1 THEN json_type(NULL) ELSE v END;",'UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE id BETWEEN 2 AND 4;', 'UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE id=99999;', 'UPDATE t SET w=w+1 WHERE id=2;']
+    for mode in ['--verified-file','--verified-wal-file']:
+     for i,sql in enumerate(sqls):
+      case=folder/(mode+str(i));case.mkdir();left=case/'lean.db';right=case/'c.db';shutil.copyfile(base,left);shutil.copyfile(base,right);bl,br=snap(left),snap(right)
+      l=run([str(binary),mode,str(left)],sql);c=csql(right,sql);al,ar=snap(left),snap(right)
+      obs='PRAGMA integrity_check;SELECT id,typeof(v),hex(v),typeof(w),hex(w) FROM t ORDER BY id;';lo,co=csql(left,obs),csql(right,obs)
+      same=bool(l['code'])==bool(c['code']) and l['stdout']==c['stdout'] and err(l)==err(c) and lo['code']==co['code']==0 and lo['stdout']==co['stdout'] and lo['stdout'].startswith('ok\n') and (bl==al)==(br==ar)
+      cases.append(dict(fixture=fixture,index=ix,page_size=page,mode=mode,sql=sql,lean=l,sqlite=c,lean_error=err(l),sqlite_error=err(c),observation_lean=lo,observation_sqlite=co,lean_unchanged=bl==al,sqlite_unchanged=br==ar,matched=same))
